- **genealogy-mode**: Use when the user wants to start a genealogy case, research ancestors or descendants, or resume family-history work; plans and routes to specialists. Not for code trees or DNA kit shopping. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-mode) · ★1)
- **genealogy-narrative**: Use when the user asks for /story or an ancestor's life told as a readable narrative, keeping records, inference and invented scenes apart. Not for finding records or proving facts. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-narrative) · ★1)
- **genealogy-oral-history**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user plans or analyses family interviews, letters, photos or inherited stories. Not for the first tree interview, which genealogy-interview runs. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-oral-history) · ★1)
- **genealogy-origin-source-discovery**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user meets a new birthplace, origin or migration and needs the country's sources mapped. Not for searching a known collection's entries. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-origin-source-discovery) · ★1)
- **genealogy-proof-evaluation**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user must judge whether an identity, parentage, event or origin claim meets the evidence standard. Not for gathering more records. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-proof-evaluation) · ★1)
- **genealogy-read-manuscripts**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user needs handwritten records read with four separate reading methods and reconciled. Not for typed text or a quick first pass over long files. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-read-manuscripts) · ★1)
- **genealogy-record-acquisition**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user needs a record downloaded, browsed page by page or ordered, with provenance kept. Not for deciding which collection to search. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-record-acquisition) · ★1)
- **genealogy-reporting**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user wants findings written up as cited reports, timelines, trees or handoffs. Not for literary life stories, which genealogy-narrative writes. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-reporting) · ★1)
- **genealogy-repository-discovery**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user needs the archives, registries or libraries that hold records for a place and period. Not for writing to them or searching their catalogues. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-repository-discovery) · ★1)
- **genealogy-research**: Use when the user works on family history: shares scans, photos, or database screenshots of parish, civil, census, military, or migration records; asks about ancestors, surnames, or a family tree; edits or validates a GEDCOM file; keeps genealogical notes in an Obsidian vault; or needs archives, databases, APIs, or lawful retrieval methods for a country, region, or time period. Any language or script, including handwritten historical records. ([sliday/genealogy-research](https://github.com/sliday/genealogy-research/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★43)
- **genealogy-research**: Genealogical research, census records, and family tree building ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-research) · ★2)
- **genealogy-research-maintenance**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user needs case evidence kept consistent after discoveries, merges, imports or corrections. Not for planning the next search. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-research-maintenance) · ★1)
- **genealogy-research-method**: Use this when researching, grading, or reporting on any family line or ancestor claim: free sources first, proof-ladder grading, a claims table, and owner-approved asks only. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/research-briefings/lineage-desk-by-eclipse-engine/skills/genealogy-research-method) · ★6)
- **genealogy-research-planner**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user needs the next search chosen, competing explanations tested or a stalled case unblocked. Not for running the searches themselves. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-research-planner) · ★1)
- **genealogy-research-planner**: Plan, prioritise and log genealogical research. From a person, couple or question (in a GEDCOM file, notes or a description), states the research question precisely, inventories what is proven, determines which records exist for that place and period and where they are held or online, orders the searches by what each would prove, applies brick-wall strategies (name variants, collateral lines, witnesses and neighbours, substitutes for lost registers), and keeps a research log with negative results so searches are not repeated. Covers France in depth plus Belgium/Flanders and Poland/Prussia, in… ([pthalamy/skills](https://github.com/pthalamy/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-research-planner))
- **genealogy-research-recipes**: Investigate genealogy questions with DNA matches and historical records. Use when recovering an ancestor’s identity, parentage or place of origin; reconciling conflicting records or DNA results; searching archives, record sites or family papers; interpreting genetic comparisons; or preparing citations, research logs and proof arguments. Includes platform workflows and boundaries for living people, data transfers and contact. ([Kinprove/genealogy-research-recipes](https://github.com/Kinprove/genealogy-research-recipes/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-research-recipes) · ★32)
- **genealogy-search-russia**: Use when someone wants to find ancestors or build a family tree (родословная, генеалогия, найти прадеда, дед воевал, раскулачили, репрессирован, «Память народа») with roots in Russia, the Russian Empire or the USSR — metric books, revision lists, ZAGS, Soviet records, archive portals and requests, reading old handwriting, judging whether a record is the right person, or before concluding «записи нет» or «это точно наши». If the project already has its own dedicated genealogy skill, prefer that one. ([nicolaspolov/genealogy-search-russia](https://github.com/nicolaspolov/genealogy-search-russia/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **genealogy-site-publisher**: Update a public family-history site from canonical genealogy research while protecting living people, preserving conflicts, grounding stories, and verifying publication. Use when a configured genealogy project needs an evidence-backed site refresh. ([Jdjohnson/skills](https://github.com/Jdjohnson/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-site-publisher))
- **genealogy-source-discovery**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user needs collections, volumes or entries found for an open question in catalogues and indexes. Not for mapping a new country's sources. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-source-discovery) · ★1)
- **genealogy-transcribe-detailed**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user needs a diplomatic reading of chosen pages using four distinct handwriting methods. Not for a fast first pass, which transcribe-quick does. ([rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills](https://github.com/rfsbraz/genealogy-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genealogy-transcribe-detailed) · ★1)
- **genealogy-transcribe-quick**: Use when genealogy-mode or the user wants a fast first reading of a long document for searchable text and leads. - **genebass-gene-burden-skill**: Submit compact Genebass gene burden requests for one Ensembl gene ID and one burden set. - **geneblocks**: Compare DNA sequences, find common blocks, highlight sequence differences, and transfer GenBank features between records using Geneblocks. - **genecards-gene-annotation-querying**: Use when a gene list (e.g. 16,000 HVGs from FindVariableFeatures(), variance, scranpy and M3drop) must be classified by database annotation into communication-related vs. function-only categories before downstream filtering. - **genedom**: Manage the domestication of genetic parts for Golden Gate assembly standards (EMMA, MoClo, etc.) using Genedom. ([fmenol/cuba-synbio-skills](https://github.com/fmenol/cuba-synbio-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genedom))
- **geneformer**: geneformer is a context-aware, attention-based deep learning model pre-trained on large-scale human single-cell transcriptome data (Genecorpus-30M). Use this model when you need to perform gene classification, network biology predictions, or analyze gene expression patterns in single-cell data. - **genel-bakis**: Avukatlık Hukuku ve Meslek Kuralları eklentisine giriş, hızlı triyaj ve iş akışı yönlendirmesi. Rol, hedef, süre, belge, risk ve istenen çıktıyı sorar; bu eklentideki uygun uzman becerileri önerir ve net bir çalışma planına bağlar. Bağlam yazısı olmadan belge yüklendiğinde bağımsız tepki verir: materyali sınıflar, süre/aciliyet taraması yapar, uygun uzman beceriye yönlendirir ya da tek bir belirleyici soru sorar.
